    179             1                    ***0***                 tx.row_a_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 3)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd100;
    180                                              
    181             1                    ***0***                 tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 0)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd9;
    182             1                    ***0***                 tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 1)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd15;
    183             1                    ***0***                 tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 2)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd12;
    184             1                    ***0***                 tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 3)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd3;
    185                                                      end
    186                                              
    187             1                    ***0***             tx.inject_fault  = 1'b1;
    188             1                    ***0***             tx.fault_pe_id   = 6'd63;  // PE[7][7]
    189             1                    ***0***             tx.fault_bit_pos = 6'd10;  // flip bit 10
    190             1                    ***0***             tx.fault_cycle   = 10;
    191                                              
    192             1                    ***0***             finish_item(tx);
    193                                              	    endtask
    194                                              
    195                                              	endclass
    196                                              
    197                                              	class accel_random_fault_seq extends uvm_sequence #(accel_txn);
    198                                              
    199             1                    ***0***     	    `uvm_object_utils(accel_random_fault_seq)
    199             2                    ***0***     
    199             3                    ***0***     
    199             4                    ***0***     
    199             5                    ***0***     
    199             6                    ***0***     
    199             7                    ***0***     
    199             8                    ***0***     
    199             9                    ***0***     
    199            10                    ***0***     
    200                                              
    201                                               	   function new(string name = "accel_random_fault_seq");
    202             1                    ***0***      	       super.new(name);
    203                                               	   endfunction
    204                                              
    205                                               	   virtual task body();
    206                                               	       accel_txn tx;
    207                                              
    208             1                    ***0***       	      repeat (10) begin
    209             1                    ***0***     	            tx = accel_txn::type_id::create("tx_random_fault");
    210                                              
    211             1                    ***0***                 start_item(tx);
    212                                              
    213             1                    ***0***                 for (int i = 0; i < N; i++) begin
    213             2                    ***0***     
    214             1                    ***0***                     tx.row_a_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 0)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd7;
    215             1                    ***0***                     tx.row_a_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 1)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd15;
    216             1                    ***0***                     tx.row_a_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 2)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd25;
    217             1                    ***0***                     tx.row_a_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 3)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd100;
    218                                              
    219             1                    ***0***                     tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 0)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd9;
    220             1                    ***0***                     tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 1)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd15;
    221             1                    ***0***                     tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 2)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd12;
    222             1                    ***0***                     tx.col_b_in_flat[((i*MAC_PER_PE + 3)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH] = 8'd3;
    223                                                          end
    224                                              
    225             1                    ***0***                 tx.inject_fault  = 1'b1;
    226             1                    ***0***                 tx.fault_pe_id   = $urandom_range(0, 63);
    227             1                    ***0***                 tx.fault_bit_pos = $urandom_range(0, 33);
    228             1                    ***0***                 tx.fault_cycle   = $urandom_range(5, 20);
    229                                              
    230             1                    ***0***                 finish_item(tx);
    231                                              	        end
    232                                              	    endtask
    233                                              
    234                                              	endclass
    235                                              
    236

    251                                                  // ============================================================
    252                                                  // 5. Driver
    253                                                  // ============================================================
    254                                                  class accel_driver extends uvm_driver #(accel_txn);
    255                                              
    256             1                    ***0***             `uvm_component_utils(accel_driver)
    256             2                    ***0***     
    256             3                    ***0***     
    257                                              
    258                                                      virtual accel_if.drv_mp vif;
    259                                              
    260                                                      function new(string name = "accel_driver", uvm_component parent = null);
    261             1                          1                 super.new(name, parent);
    262                                                      endfunction
    263                                              
    264                                                      function void build_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    265             1                          1                 super.build_phase(phase);
    266                                              
    267                                                          if (!uvm_config_db#(virtual accel_if.drv_mp)::get(this, "", "vif", vif)) begin
    268             1                    ***0***                     `uvm_fatal("NOVIF", "Driver virtual interface not found")
    269                                                          end
    270                                                      endfunction
    271                                              
    272                                                     task reset_dut();
    273             1                          1     	    vif.rst_n         <= 1'b0;
    274             1                          1     	    vif.start         <= 1'b0;
    275             1                          1     	    vif.clear_acc     <= 1'b0;
    276             1                          1     	    vif.row_a_in_flat <= '0;
    277             1                          1     	    vif.col_b_in_flat <= '0;
    278                                              
    279             1                          1     	    vif.fault_en      <= 1'b0;
    280             1                          1     	    vif.fault_pe_id   <= 6'd0;
    281             1                          1     	    vif.fault_bit_pos <= 6'd0;
    282                                              
    283             1                          1     	    vif.fault_was_injected      <= 1'b0;
    284             1                          1     	    vif.injected_fault_pe_id    <= 6'd0;
    285             1                          1     	    vif.injected_fault_bit_pos  <= 6'd0;
    286                                              
    287             1                          5     	    repeat (5) @(posedge vif.clk);
    287             2                          5     
    288                                              
    289             1                          1     	    vif.rst_n <= 1'b1;
    290                                              
    291             1                          2     	    repeat (2) @(posedge vif.clk);
    291             2                          2     
    292                                              
    293             1                          1     	    vif.clear_acc <= 1'b1;
    294             1                          1     	    @(posedge vif.clk);
    295             1                          1     	    vif.clear_acc <= 1'b0;
    296                                              
    297             1                          1     	    `uvm_info("DRV", "DUT reset completed", UVM_LOW)
    298                                              	endtask
    299                                              
    300                                                    task drive_one_txn(accel_txn tx);
    301                                                  int cycle;
    302                                              
    303             1                          1         @(posedge vif.clk);
    304                                              
    305             1                          1         vif.row_a_in_flat <= tx.row_a_in_flat;
    306             1                          1         vif.col_b_in_flat <= tx.col_b_in_flat;
    307                                              
    308             1                          1         vif.fault_en      <= 1'b0;
    309             1                          1         vif.fault_pe_id   <= tx.fault_pe_id;
    310             1                          1         vif.fault_bit_pos <= tx.fault_bit_pos;
    311                                              
    312             1                          1         vif.fault_was_injected      <= 1'b0;
    313             1                          1         vif.injected_fault_pe_id    <= tx.fault_pe_id;
    314             1                          1         vif.injected_fault_bit_pos  <= tx.fault_bit_pos;
    315                                              
    316             1                          1         @(posedge vif.clk);
    317             1                          1         vif.start <= 1'b1;
    318                                              
    319             1                          1         @(posedge vif.clk);
    320             1                          1         vif.start <= 1'b0;
    321                                              
    322             1                          1         `uvm_info("DRV",
    323                                                      $sformatf("Transaction started. inject_fault=%0b pe_id=%0d bit=%0d cycle=%0d",
    324                                                                tx.inject_fault, tx.fault_pe_id, tx.fault_bit_pos, tx.fault_cycle),
    325                                                      UVM_MEDIUM)
    326                                              
    327             1                          1         cycle = 0;
    328                                              
    329             1                         18         while (vif.done !== 1'b1) begin
    330             1                         17             @(posedge vif.clk);
    331             1                         17             cycle++;
    332                                              
    333                                                      if (tx.inject_fault && cycle == tx.fault_cycle) begin
    334             1                          1                 vif.fault_en      <= 1'b1;
    335             1                          1                 vif.fault_pe_id   <= tx.fault_pe_id;
    336             1                          1                 vif.fault_bit_pos <= tx.fault_bit_pos;
    337                                              
    338             1                          1                 vif.fault_was_injected      <= 1'b1;
    339             1                          1                 vif.injected_fault_pe_id    <= tx.fault_pe_id;
    340             1                          1                 vif.injected_fault_bit_pos  <= tx.fault_bit_pos;
    341                                              
    342             1                          1                 `uvm_info("FAULT_DRV",
    343                                                              $sformatf("Injected fault: PE_ID=%0d BIT=%0d at cycle=%0d",
    344                                                                        tx.fault_pe_id, tx.fault_bit_pos, cycle),
    345                                                              UVM_LOW)
    346                                                      end else begin
    347             1                         16                 vif.fault_en <= 1'b0;
    348                                                      end
    349                                                  end
    350                                              
    351             1                          1         vif.fault_en <= 1'b0;
    352                                              
    353             1                          1         @(posedge vif.clk);
    354                                              endtask
    355                                              
    356                                                      virtual task run_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    357                                                          accel_txn tx;
    358                                              
    359             1                          1                 reset_dut();
    360                                              
    361             1                          1                 forever begin
    362             1                          2                     seq_item_port.get_next_item(tx);
    363             1                          1                     drive_one_txn(tx);
    364             1                          1                     seq_item_port.item_done();
    365                                                          end
    366                                                      endtask
    367                                              
    368                                                  endclass
    369                                              
    370                                              
    371                                                  // ============================================================
    372                                                  // 6. Monitor
    373                                                  // ============================================================
    374                                                  class accel_monitor extends uvm_component;
    375                                              
    376             1                    ***0***             `uvm_component_utils(accel_monitor)
    376             2                    ***0***     
    376             3                    ***0***     
    377                                              
    378                                                      virtual accel_if.mon_mp vif;
    379                                                      uvm_analysis_port #(accel_txn) mon_ap;
    380                                              
    381                                                      function new(string name = "accel_monitor", uvm_component parent = null);
    382             1                          1                 super.new(name, parent);
    383             1                          1                 mon_ap = new("mon_ap", this);
    384                                                      endfunction
    385                                              
    386                                                      function void build_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    387             1                          1                 super.build_phase(phase);
    388                                              
    389                                                          if (!uvm_config_db#(virtual accel_if.mon_mp)::get(this, "", "vif", vif)) begin
    390             1                    ***0***                     `uvm_fatal("NOVIF", "Monitor virtual interface not found")
    391                                                          end
    392                                                      endfunction
    393                                              
    394                                                      virtual task run_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    395                                              	    accel_txn tx;
    396                                              
    397             1                          1     	    forever begin
    398             1                         80     	        @(posedge vif.clk);
    399                                              
    400                                              	        if (vif.done && vif.result_valid) begin
    401             1                          1     	    tx = accel_txn::type_id::create("tx_mon");
    402                                              
    403             1                          1     	    tx.row_a_in_flat = vif.row_a_in_flat;
    404             1                          1     	    tx.col_b_in_flat = vif.col_b_in_flat;
    405             1                          1     	    tx.pe_sum_flat   = vif.pe_sum_flat;
    406             1                          1     	    tx.pe_valid_flat = vif.pe_valid_flat;
    407             1                          1     	    tx.done          = vif.done;
    408             1                          1     	    tx.result_valid  = vif.result_valid;
    409             1                          1     	    tx.cycle_count   = vif.cycle_count;
    410                                              
    411             1                          1     	    tx.inject_fault  = vif.fault_was_injected;
    412             1                          1     	    tx.fault_pe_id   = vif.injected_fault_pe_id;
    413             1                          1     	    tx.fault_bit_pos = vif.injected_fault_bit_pos;
    414             1                          1     	    tx.fault_cycle   = vif.cycle_count;
    415                                              
    416             1                          1     	    `uvm_info("MON", "Captured DUT output transaction", UVM_MEDIUM)
    417                                              	
    418             1                          1     	    mon_ap.write(tx);
    419                                              		end
    420                                              	    end
    421                                              	endtask
    422                                                  endclass
    423                                              
    424                                              
    425                                              // ============================================================
    426                                              // 7. Scoreboard
    427                                              // ============================================================
    428                                              class accel_scoreboard extends uvm_component;
    429                                              
    430             1                    ***0***         `uvm_component_utils(accel_scoreboard)
    430             2                    ***0***     
    430             3                    ***0***     
    431                                              
    432                                                  uvm_analysis_imp #(accel_txn, accel_scoreboard) sb_imp;
    433                                              
    434                                                  int pass_count;
    435                                                  int fail_count;
    436                                                  int mismatch_count;
    437                                              
    438                                                  longint unsigned total_error;
    439                                                  longint unsigned max_error;
    440                                                  longint unsigned local_error;
    441                                              
    442                                                  int injected_fault_count;
    443                                                  int detected_fault_count;
    444                                              
    445                                                  real fault_coverage;
    446                                                  real error_per_fault;
    447                                              
    448                                                  function new(string name = "accel_scoreboard", uvm_component parent = null);
    449             1                          1             super.new(name, parent);
    450             1                          1             sb_imp = new("sb_imp", this);
    451                                              
    452             1                          1             pass_count           = 0;
    453             1                          1             fail_count           = 0;
    454             1                          1             mismatch_count       = 0;
    455             1                          1             total_error          = 0;
    456             1                          1             max_error            = 0;
    457             1                          1             local_error          = 0;
    458             1                          1             injected_fault_count = 0;
    459             1                          1             detected_fault_count = 0;
    460             1                          1             fault_coverage       = 0.0;
    461             1                          1             error_per_fault      = 0.0;
    462                                                  endfunction
    463                                              
    464                                              
    465                                                  function bit [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] get_pe_sum(
    466                                                      input bit [OUT_WIDTH-1:0] flat,
    467                                                      input int row,
    468                                                      input int col
    469                                                  );
    470             1                         64             get_pe_sum = flat[((row*N + col)*PE_SUM_WIDTH) +: PE_SUM_WIDTH];
    471                                                  endfunction
    472                                              
    473                                              
    474                                                 function bit [15:0] approx_mult(
    475                                                  input bit [7:0] a,
    476                                                  input bit [7:0] b
    477                                              );
    478                                                  bit [15:0] exact;
    479                                                  bit [15:0] approx;
    480                                                  bit [15:0] compensation;
    481                                              
    482                                                  begin
    483             1                        256             exact = a * b;
    484                                              
    485                                                      // Truncate lower bits
    486             1                        256             approx = exact;
    487             1                        256             approx[APPROX_BITS-1:0] = '0;
    488                                              
    489                                                      // Match RTL compensation:
    490                                                      // APPROX_BITS = 4 gives compensation = 8
    491             1                        256             compensation = 16'd1 << (APPROX_BITS - 1);
    492                                              
    493             1                        256             approx = approx + compensation;
    494                                              
    495             1                        256             return approx;
    496                                                  end
    497                                              endfunction
    498                                              
    499                                              
    500                                                  function bit [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] expected_pe_value(
    501                                                      input bit [IN_WIDTH-1:0] row_a,
    502                                                      input bit [IN_WIDTH-1:0] col_b,
    503                                                      input int row,
    504                                                      input int col
    505                                                  );
    506                                                      bit [7:0] a0, a1, a2, a3;
    507                                                      bit [7:0] b0, b1, b2, b3;
    508                                                      bit [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] exp;
    509                                              
    510                                                      begin
    511             1                         64                 a0 = row_a[((row*MAC_PER_PE + 0)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    512             1                         64                 a1 = row_a[((row*MAC_PER_PE + 1)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    513             1                         64                 a2 = row_a[((row*MAC_PER_PE + 2)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    514             1                         64                 a3 = row_a[((row*MAC_PER_PE + 3)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    515                                              
    516             1                         64                 b0 = col_b[((col*MAC_PER_PE + 0)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    517             1                         64                 b1 = col_b[((col*MAC_PER_PE + 1)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    518             1                         64                 b2 = col_b[((col*MAC_PER_PE + 2)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    519             1                         64                 b3 = col_b[((col*MAC_PER_PE + 3)*DATA_WIDTH) +: DATA_WIDTH];
    520                                              
    521             1                         64                 exp = approx_mult(a0, b0)
    522                                                              + approx_mult(a1, b1)
    523                                                              + approx_mult(a2, b2)
    524                                                              + approx_mult(a3, b3);
    525                                              
    526             1                         64                 return exp;
    527                                                      end
    528                                                  endfunction
    529                                              
    530                                              
    531                                                  function void write(accel_txn tx);
    532                                                  bit [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] actual;
    533                                                  bit [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] expected;
    534                                                  bit fault_detected_this_txn;
    535                                              
    536             1                          1         `uvm_info("SB", "Scoreboard received transaction", UVM_LOW)
    537                                              
    538             1                          1         fault_detected_this_txn = 1'b0;
    539                                              
    540                                                  if (tx.done !== 1'b1 || tx.result_valid !== 1'b1) begin
    541             1                    ***0***             `uvm_error("SB", "done/result_valid protocol failure")
    542             1                    ***0***             fail_count++;
    543             1                    ***0***             return;
    544                                                  end
    545                                              
    546                                                  if (&tx.pe_valid_flat !== 1'b1) begin
    547             1                    ***0***             `uvm_error("SB", "Not all PE valid bits are high")
    548             1                    ***0***             fail_count++;
    549                                                  end
    550                                              
    551                                                  if (tx.inject_fault) begin
    552             1                          1             injected_fault_count++;
    553                                                  end
    554                                              
    555             1                          1         for (int r = 0; r < N; r++) begin
    555             2                          8     
    556             1                          8             for (int c = 0; c < N; c++) begin
    556             2                         64     
    557                                              
    558             1                         64                 actual   = get_pe_sum(tx.pe_sum_flat, r, c);
    559             1                         64                 expected = expected_pe_value(tx.row_a_in_flat, tx.col_b_in_flat, r, c);
    560                                              
    561                                                          if (actual === expected) begin
    562             1                    ***0***                     pass_count++;
    563                                                          end else begin
    564             1                         64                     mismatch_count++;
    565             1                         64                     fault_detected_this_txn = 1'b1;
    566                                              
    567                                                              if (actual > expected)
    568             1                          1                         local_error = actual - expected;
    569                                                              else
    570             1                         63                         local_error = expected - actual;
    571                                              
    572             1                         64                     total_error += local_error;
    573                                              
    574                                                              if (local_error > max_error)
    575             1                          2                         max_error = local_error;
    576                                              
    577                                                              if (tx.inject_fault) begin
    578             1                         64                         `uvm_info("SB_FAULT",
    579                                                                      $sformatf("Expected fault effect PE[%0d][%0d]: actual=%0d expected=%0d error=%0d",
    580                                                                                r, c, actual, expected, local_error),
    581                                                                      UVM_LOW)
    582                                                              end else begin
    583             1                    ***0***                         fail_count++;
    584                                              
    585             1                    ***0***                         `uvm_error("SB",
    586                                                                      $sformatf("Unexpected mismatch PE[%0d][%0d]: actual=%0d expected=%0d error=%0d",
    587                                                                                r, c, actual, expected, local_error))
    588                                                              end
    589                                                          end
    590                                                      end
    591                                                  end
    592                                              
    593                                                  if (tx.inject_fault && fault_detected_this_txn) begin
    594             1                          1             detected_fault_count++;
    595                                                  end
    596                                              
    597                                                  if (injected_fault_count > 0) begin
    598             1                          1             fault_coverage  = (detected_fault_count * 100.0) / injected_fault_count;
    599             1                          1             error_per_fault = total_error * 1.0 / injected_fault_count;
    600                                                  end else begin
    601             1                    ***0***             fault_coverage  = 0.0;
    602             1                    ***0***             error_per_fault = 0.0;
    603                                                  end
    604                                              
    605             1                          1         `uvm_info("SB",
    606                                                      $sformatf("PASS=%0d FAIL=%0d MISMATCH=%0d TOTAL_ERROR=%0d MAX_ERROR=%0d",
    607                                                                pass_count, fail_count, mismatch_count, total_error, max_error),
    608                                                      UVM_LOW)
    609                                              
    610                                              endfunction
    611                                              
    612                                              
    613                                                  function void report_phase(uvm_phase phase);
    614             1                          1             super.report_phase(phase);
    615                                              
    616             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "=================================================", UVM_NONE)
    617             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "Final Result Analysis Report", UVM_NONE)
    618             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "=================================================", UVM_NONE)
    619                                              
    620             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    621                                                          $sformatf("PASS count           = %0d", pass_count),
    622                                                          UVM_NONE)
    623                                              
    624             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    625                                                          $sformatf("FAIL count           = %0d", fail_count),
    626                                                          UVM_NONE)
    627                                              
    628             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    629                                                          $sformatf("Mismatch count       = %0d", mismatch_count),
    630                                                          UVM_NONE)
    631                                              
    632             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    633                                                          $sformatf("Total error          = %0d", total_error),
    634                                                          UVM_NONE)
    635                                              
    636             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    637                                                          $sformatf("Maximum PE error     = %0d", max_error),
    638                                                          UVM_NONE)
    639                                              
    640             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    641                                                          $sformatf("Injected faults      = %0d", injected_fault_count),
    642                                                          UVM_NONE)
    643                                              
    644             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    645                                                          $sformatf("Detected faults      = %0d", detected_fault_count),
    646                                                          UVM_NONE)
    647                                              
    648             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    649                                                          $sformatf("Fault coverage       = %0.2f %%", fault_coverage),
    650                                                          UVM_NONE)
    651                                              
    652             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT",
    653                                                          $sformatf("Error per fault      = %0.2f", error_per_fault),
    654                                                          UVM_NONE)
    655                                              
    656             1                          1             `uvm_info("PYTHON_RESULT",
    657                                                          $sformatf("MISMATCH=%0d TOTAL_ERROR=%0d MAX_ERROR=%0d INJECTED=%0d DETECTED=%0d",
    658                                                                    mismatch_count,
    659                                                                    total_error,
    660                                                                    max_error,
    661                                                                    injected_fault_count,
    662                                                                    detected_fault_count),
    663                                                          UVM_NONE)
    664                                              
    665             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "=================================================", UVM_NONE)
    666                                              
    667                                                      if (injected_fault_count == 0 && fail_count == 0) begin
    668             1                    ***0***                 `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "NORMAL TEST RESULT: PASSED", UVM_NONE)
    669                                                      end else if (injected_fault_count > 0 && mismatch_count > 0) begin
    670             1                          1                 `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "FAULT TEST RESULT: FAULT EFFECT DETECTED", UVM_NONE)
    671                                                      end else if (injected_fault_count > 0 && mismatch_count == 0) begin
    672             1                    ***0***                 `uvm_warning("FINAL_REPORT", "FAULT TEST RESULT: FAULT MASKED / NOT DETECTED")
    673                                                      end else begin
    674             1                    ***0***                 `uvm_error("FINAL_REPORT", "TEST FAILED")
    675                                                      end
    676                                              
    677             1                          1             `uvm_info("FINAL_REPORT", "=================================================", UVM_NONE)
    678                                                  endfunction
    679                                              
    680                                              endclass

    5                                                    parameter DATA_WIDTH  = 8,
    6                                                    parameter ACC_WIDTH   = 32,
    7                                                    parameter APPROX_BITS = 4,
    8                                                    parameter MAC_PER_PE  = 4
    9                                                )(
    10                                                   input  logic                                  clk,
    11                                                   input  logic                                  rst_n,
    12                                               
    13                                                   input  logic                                  start,
    14                                                   input  logic                                  clear_acc,
    15                                               
    16                                                   input  logic [(N*MAC_PER_PE*DATA_WIDTH)-1:0] row_a_in_flat,
    17                                                   input  logic [(N*MAC_PER_PE*DATA_WIDTH)-1:0] col_b_in_flat,
    18                                               
    19                                                   input  logic                                  fault_en,
    20                                                   input  logic [5:0]                            fault_pe_id,
    21                                                   input  logic [5:0]                            fault_bit_pos,
    22                                               
    23                                                   output logic [(N*N*(ACC_WIDTH+2))-1:0]        pe_sum_flat,
    24                                                   output logic [(N*N)-1:0]                      pe_valid_flat,
    25                                               
    26                                                   output logic                                  result_valid,
    27                                                   output logic                                  done,
    28                                                   output logic [7:0]                            cycle_count
    29                                               );
    30                                               
    31                                                   localparam PE_SUM_WIDTH  = ACC_WIDTH + 2;
    32                                                   localparam OUT_WIDTH     = N * N * PE_SUM_WIDTH;
    33                                                   localparam ARRAY_LATENCY = (2 * N) + 6;
    34                                               
    35                                                   logic [N-1:0] row_valid_in;
    36                                                   logic [N-1:0] col_valid_in;
    37                                               
    38                                                   logic running;
    39

    41                                               
    42                                                   logic [(N*N)-1:0] raw_pe_valid_flat;
    43                                                   logic [(N*N)-1:0] valid_seen_vec;
    44                                               
    45                                                   logic        fault_active_reg;
    46                                                   logic [5:0]  fault_pe_id_reg;
    47                                                   logic [5:0]  fault_bit_pos_reg;
    48                                               
    49                                                   // ------------------------------------------------------------
    50                                                   // Internal 8x8 systolic array
    51                                                   // ------------------------------------------------------------
    52                                                   systolic_array_nxn #(
    53                                                       .N(N),
    54                                                       .DATA_WIDTH(DATA_WIDTH),
    55                                                       .ACC_WIDTH(ACC_WIDTH),
    56                                                       .APPROX_BITS(APPROX_BITS),
    57                                                       .MAC_PER_PE(MAC_PER_PE)
    58                                                   ) u_systolic_array (
    59                                                       .clk(clk),
    60                                                       .rst_n(rst_n),
    61                                                       .clear_acc(clear_acc),
    62                                               
    63                                                       .row_valid_in(row_valid_in),
    64                                                       .col_valid_in(col_valid_in),
    65                                               
    66                                                       .row_a_in_flat(row_a_in_flat),
    67                                                       .col_b_in_flat(col_b_in_flat),
    68                                               
    69                                                       .pe_sum_flat(clean_pe_sum_flat),
    70                                                       .pe_valid_flat(raw_pe_valid_flat)
    71                                                   );
    72                                               
    73                                                   // ------------------------------------------------------------
    74                                                   // Per-PE fault injection output mapping
    75                                                   // Only the selected PE output field is XORed with a local mask.
    76                                                   // This avoids accidentally applying one bit flip to all 64 PEs.
    77                                                   // ------------------------------------------------------------
    78                                                   genvar fp;
    79                                               
    80                                                   generate
    81                                                       for (fp = 0; fp < N*N; fp = fp + 1) begin : FAULT_OUT_GEN
    82                                               
    83                                                           logic [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] clean_pe_word;
    84                                                           logic [PE_SUM_WIDTH-1:0] faulted_pe_word;

    88                                                               clean_pe_sum_flat[(fp*PE_SUM_WIDTH) +: PE_SUM_WIDTH];
    89

    94                                                                   if (fault_bit_pos_reg < PE_SUM_WIDTH) begin

    96                                                                   end
    97                                                               end
    98                                                           end
    99                                               
    100             1                        257                 assign faulted_pe_word = clean_pe_word ^ local_fault_mask;
    101                                              
    102                                                          assign pe_sum_flat[(fp*PE_SUM_WIDTH) +: PE_SUM_WIDTH] =
    103                                                              faulted_pe_word;
    104                                              
    105                                                      end
    106                                                  endgenerate
    107                                              
    108                                                  // ------------------------------------------------------------
    109                                                  // Latched PE valid vector
    110                                                  // raw_pe_valid_flat pulses at different times for different PEs.
    111                                                  // valid_seen_vec remembers which PEs have already produced valid output.
    112                                                  // ------------------------------------------------------------
    113                                                  assign pe_valid_flat = valid_seen_vec;
    114                                              
    115                                                  // ------------------------------------------------------------
    116                                                  // Systolic skewed valid generation
    117                                                  //
    118                                                  // For PE[i][j]:
    119                                                  // activation reaches after j cycles
    120                                                  // weight reaches after i cycles
    121                                                  //
    122                                                  // To make valid_a and valid_b meet at every PE:
    123                                                  // row_valid_in[i] is launched at cycle i
    124                                                  // col_valid_in[j] is launched at cycle j
